### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7300

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.50-159.00 
前週交易區間 
156.35 158.45

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，EUR/USD 震盪下
行至 1.1310，而在紐約聯儲主席威廉姆斯發表不急於再次升息的偏鴿言論後，
EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1340；週間，美國 8 月 PCE 數據低於預期，EUR/USD 一度反彈
至 1.1380，然而在全球債券拋售潮下，資金回流美元帶動美元指數突破 102 關卡，
EUR/USD 跌破 1.1300 關卡最低來至 1.1214；臨近週末，美國 9 月非農就業人數低
於預期，EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1285 後回落，終場收在 1.1253，週線下跌 138 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元地區消費者
物價調和指數(HICP)，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%、歐元區年增 3.8%，顯示能
源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下， 市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息 ， 將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人 ， 失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1400 
前週交易區間 
1.1214 1.1391

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.29-115.10.02 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週為提存期底 ， 繼央行上週大砍存單效應持續發酵 ， 市
場資金供給仍充裕，惟需求疲弱，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投仍冷清，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三於處所成交在 1.82%，10 年
期指標券則未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價為 1.95%。 
第四季台債發行量雖偏少 ， 惟美伊和談依舊陷入僵局 ， 國際油價續處高位 ， 通
膨疑慮尚存，國際債市走勢仍偏空，預期台債殖利率短線將維持高檔盤整格
局，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.95%~2%。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。

最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震

於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。

  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

1 群益投顧 
台達電 (2308 TT) 公布 3Q26 營收為 1,970.59 億元、 QoQ+7.5% ； 因 提 列 庫 存 跌 價 損 失
影 響，毛 利 率 降 至 34.1% ，營 業 利 益 率 為 16.6% ；稅 後 淨 利 為 234.95 億 元、QoQ -6.5% ，
稅後 EPS 為 9.04 元 。 隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、 Vera Rubin 產品 量產出貨 ， 預期 4Q26 營運
將有顯著提升 。 受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ，
投資評等為 Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.0 4 元 ：  
台 達 電 公 布 3Q26 營 收 為 1, 970.59 億 元 、 QoQ+ 7.5%； 因 提 列 庫 存 跌 價 損 失
影響 ， 毛 利 率 降 至 34.1% ， 營 業 利 益 率 為 16.6 %； 稅 後 淨 利 為 234.9 5 億 元 、
QoQ -6.5 %， 稅 後 EPS 為 9. 04 元 ， 低 於 預 期 。  
產 品 規 格 提 升 ， 電源 類 產 品 展 望 佳：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。800 VDC 與 ± 400VDC 預 計 於 4Q26 出貨 ，
2026 年 營 收 比 重 約 1% ，預 期 2H27 營 收 貢 獻 較 顯 著。  
水 冷 散 熱 2026 年 營 收 維 持 成 長 趨 勢：  
水冷散熱產品有水冷板、 Manifold 、 CDU 、 Sidecar 。目前出貨以系統類的
Sidecar 為 主 ， 歸 類 於 基 礎 建 設； CDU 則 歸 類 於 電 源 類 與 零 組 件 。 水 冷 散 熱
2025 年 營 收 比 重 為 9%，台 達 電 Sidecar 產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高，雖 有 競 爭
者 加 入 ， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ，2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨
勢，預估 2026 年 水 冷 散 熱 營 收 比 重 為 12% 。  
固 態 變 壓 器 為 長 期 成 長 動 能：  
SST (固 態 變 壓器 )目 前 已 少 量 出 貨，客 戶 為 中 系 CSP 、800V 高 壓 直 流 電 動 車
充 電 站，亦 出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規
劃，預期  2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、Vera Rubin 產 品 量 產 出 貨，預 期 4Q26 營 運 將 有 顯 著 提 升。
受惠於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評
等為 Buy 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 10 月 29 日

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
  CPO Revenue on Track; Stay 
OW 
 CPO is on track for >US$500mn in 2027: Management mentioned the CPO has 
already started mass production  and will reach at least US$500mn in 2027. This is 
in-line with our estimates that we forecast CPO revenue to be >US$600mn in 2027.
Slightly upward revision of LEAP revenue: The company slightly raised 2026 LEAP 
(leading-edge advanced packaging) revenue from US$3.7bn to US$4bn and  2027 
from >US$7.5bn to >US$8bn, on track  to our forecast of US$9bn. It expects at least 
US$1bn of full process CoWoS-like revenue in 2027 vs. MSe of US$1.8bn.
Margin expansion is also on track: 3Q26 ATM gross margin has already surpassed 
the structural range and reached 30.6%. The company mentioned 30% would be 
the new floor and expects it to continue to expand sequentially in 2027.
3Q26 GM and 4Q26 guidance slightly beat: 3Q26 GM of 22.1% is slightly higher 
than both MSe and the consensus. 4Q26 revenue guidance of 6-8% QoQ growth 
and GM of 23-24% are both slightly higher than MSe of 5% QoQ growth and 22-23% 
GM forecast.

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Further upside surprise: In our recent CPO report, we raised WIN Semi's revenue
forecasts  to reflect stronger demand for satellite communications RF, driven by
major customer SpaceX (SPCX; covered by Adam Jonas). However, we
underestimated the favorable pricing and margin, as evidenced by the 28 Oct   3Q26
results. Reflecting the much higher gross margin, we raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028
EPS by 35%, 28% and 17%, respectively, and raise our price target 17% to  NT$700  to

capture the long-term upside from both new businesses - space communications
and AI optical networking laser.
3Q26 margin beat: Core margin reached 40.7% in 3Q26, well above our original
forecast of 29%. The company indicated space communications revenue already
represents 40% of its infrastructure business, accounting for 35-40% of total
revenue. This implies the space-related revenue is tracking at c.15% of total revenue
in 3Q. Despite a relatively  small portion of revenue, we estimate the business could

lift 3Q26 margin by 5% (half of 3Q26 increase), implying the space communications
margin could be >60%, well above our and Street expectations. V3 satellite
development will be a key growth driver into 2027. See Exhibit 1  for our analysis.
4Q26 revenue guidance looks solid: Smartphone and WiFi revenue remain stable
despite weak China smartphone demand: Win Semi now has higher exposure to
Apple iPhone through high-band PA (Broadcom) and WiFi (Murata). As such,

revenue can still grow single digit QoQ in 4Q26, despite recent weakness in China
smartphone demand. Please see Exhibit 3  and Exhibit 4    for  our earnings review.
Upgrade to OW: The stock trades at 25x our new 2028 EPS. Given the significant
revenue contribution from space communications and AI lasers, we think our
implied target multiple of 29x 2028 EPS should be justified. Win Semi historically
traded at 40x PE during the smartphone 3D sensing product cycle.

### Page 2

2
Surprising Margin Expansion from Optical/Space Businesses
WIN Semi   could benefit from PD growth and potential LD outsourcing
by late 2027
In our previous WIN Semi report, we argued that Lumentum (LITE.O, covered by Meta
Marshall) was unlikely to outsource CW lasers or EML production to WIN Semi, a key
pillar of our earlier UW rating. However, our recent  checks with WIN Semi management
suggest that WIN Semi and its major customer, we assume it's Broadcom (AVGO, covered

by Joseph Moore), have secured sufficient InP substrate supply for WIN Semi's PD
production, potentially supporting substantial growth for the PD (photo diode) business
in 2H26. We estimate PD could account for >10% of WIN Semi's total revenue. Our recent
checks from the supply chain also suggest that WIN Semi's largest PD customer  could
outsource LD (laser diode) production to WIN Semi.
We also see the potential for even stronger revenue growth from WIN Semi's aerospace

business into 2027, especially from the ground station and satellite business. With
SpaceX's  launch of Starship and Starlink V3 satellite, we expect WIN Semi's content value
per satellite   to increase significantly from Starlink v2 to v3, with potential upside from
future DTD (direct to device) satellites. The increase in content value comes not only from
higher RF chips volumes, but also from the migration from GaAs to GaN. Based on our
industry checks, the ASP for 4-inch GaN could be US$9k, compared with ~US$2.5-3k for 6-

inch GaAs. We therefore upgrade our rating from EW to OW with the strong gross margin
performance, which we believe to be >60%.
Where we could be wrong with our OW call: We think the largest near-term swing factor
for WIN Semi is the aerospace business, which is relatively difficult to track given the
inherent risk of delays or failures in satellite launches. However, on September 28, 2026,
SpaceX successfully launched the Starship with Starlink V3 satellite. If SpaceX decides to

speed up the satellite constellation, there also could be upside to our forecasts.
All of our satellite launch numbers are based on Adam Jonas's SpaceX forecasts. Please
see his latest report: SpaceX: SpaceX/Tesla and SI's Next Act (23 Oct 2026)

3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance review - margin and earnings beat on

strong optical and aerospace demand
WIN Semi delivered a solid 3Q26 performance, with margins and earnings exceeding
expectations, supported by robust demand across its optical and aerospace businesses.
The gross margin beat MSe and consensus by 7-8ppts.  Strength in optical applications,
driven by continued investment in AI-related optical connectivity and high-speed data
transmission, alongside resilient aerospace demand, supported a favorable product mix

and improved manufacturing efficiency.
The higher contribution from these relatively high-value-added segments lifted gross
margins above expectations, driving positive operating leverage and stronger-than-
anticipated earnings. Looking ahead, we expect sustained momentum in optical
communications and aerospace applications to remain key drivers of WIN Semi's
profitability.

WIN Semi: Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base-case value) increases from NT$600 to NT$700: This also factors
in our EPS forecast changes  for 2025 to 2028.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model as we believe it best
presents the value of the company. All of our other key assumptions are unchanged,
including a cost of equity of 8.0% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free

rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate assumption of 15% and terminal growth rate of
4.0%.
Our bull-case value  increases  from NT$900 to NT$1050 and our bear-case value from NT
$300 to NT$350, implying 44x and 14x 2028e EPS, respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 143: 261030_daiwa_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_daiwa_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: MediaTek (MTK) disclosed on 30 October a strong set of 
quarterly results and guidance both beating market expectations, thanks to 
a faster-than-expected ramp in its first AI accelerator project. We walked 
away from today’s call with a firmer view on its AI business - a faster ramp 
for the first project and a broadened customer base for a new project. We 
raise our forecasts and 12M TP, reiterate our Buy (1) call and suggest that 
investors take advantage of the recent stock pullbacks for bargain-hunting.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. MTK released 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD28bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 11%/9% 
thanks to a better topline, lower opex ratio and higher non-operating gains 
(table on p.2). 3Q26 revenue finished 6%/4% above our/consensus 
estimates and topped guidance (TWD152-160bn), thanks to an earlier 
ramp of its first AI accelerators on some easing of the substrate supply 
constraint. Guidance calls for 4Q26 revenue of TWD216bn, at the midpoint 
of the range that beat our previous/consensus forecasts by 7%/3%, driven 
by the stronger ramp of the AI accelerator project which will more than 
offset muted consumer demand weakness associated with mobile phones. 
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 1-6% and expect its AI revenue to reach 
USD16bn in 2027 and USD26bn in 2028, from USD2.7bn this year.

AI appears to be running ahead. At today’s call, management centred 
elaboration on its AI business progress. 1) It raised this year’s initial 
revenue contribution of the first AI accelerators to USD2.5bn for 4Q26, 
above its previous target of USD2bn. 2) While management keeps its AI 
accelerator (ex-HBM) addressable market unchanged at USD80bn for 
2027, it now aims for a 20% market share (previous: 15-20%), meaning 
USD16bn revenue contribution, vs. our previous forecast of USD15bn. 3) It 
expects revenue contribution in 2028 from the second AI customer (not 
from China), which supports our view depicted in our Global Tech report 
issued on 27 August, where we lifted MTK’s 2028 forecasts on the second 
customer ramp. 4) MTK aims to kick off 4Q26 with a new journey for its 
operating margin, expanding all the way into 2027 for more than 20% on 
average, echoing its previous comments that, although the AI accelerator 
business is gross-margin dilutive, it is operating-margin accretive.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we finetune our 12M 
TP for MTK to TWD5,500 (previous: TWD5,200), based on our unchanged 
4-quarter forward PER of 36x. We reiterate our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risk of any AI accelerator ramps below our expectation.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 6-8% above the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street has yet to factor in its better AI ramp.

30 October 2026

Firmly on track with AI ASIC transformer

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Raise our forecasts on faster-than-expected AI ramps 
➢ Reiterate Buy (1) rating with a higher 12M TP of TWD5,500

Company profile 
Ranked No.5 in the world by 2023 fabless semiconductor revenue, MediaTek (MTK) offers diverse 
products across optical storage, digital home, smartphone, PC, ASIC, automotive and other IoT 
applications. MTK has successfully captured the industry demand cycles from optical disk drives to 
feature phones to smartphones, and now positions itself well as a platform provider to capitalise on the 
next demand cycle of the BigData/AI to help facilitate digital transformation in human lives.

### Page 5

5

MediaTek (2454 TT): 30 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
MTK’s Board of Directors (BoD) has 8 members with diverse backgrounds in foundry, IC design
and electronics. Among its BoD, 4 members are independent directors, representing 50% of total
directors. Its Chairman and CEO are not the same person, but its CEO is also one of the
directors. MTK conducts internal annual performance evaluation of the BoD, individual directors
and functional committees.
The board currently includes only one female director out of eight members, which represents
12.5% of the total. The company recognizes this gender imbalance and is actively working
toward a more inclusive and balanced board composition
Capital management 2
MTK’s dividend payout ratios in the past 5 years were above 60% with increasing cash dividend
in the past 3 years in dollar amount. In addition, MTK is committed to paying a special dividend
of TWD16/share over 2021-24 on top of its regular annual dividend. MTK is one of the leading
fabless chipmakers in the world with continued efforts in new product development for diversified
applications; therefore, it will continue to invest in its research & development. We see its
dividend payout as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
MTK’s revenue from related parties was less than 1% of its total revenue in 2024 and transaction
terms were not abnormal compared to other customers. It also had related-party transactions
with King Yuan Electronics Co., Ltd. and King Long Tech (Suzhou) Ltd for IC testing,
experimental services, and manufacturing technology services in 2021. The purchase amount
from related parties was less than 7% of its total cost of goods sold/purchases in 2024. Thus, we
do not see meaningful impact on its business operation.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
MTK has established its "Supplier Code of Conduct", which covers areas such as labour and
human rights, health and safety, environmental protection, code of ethics, and management. It
requires all suppliers to sign a guarantee to comply with this code of conduct. MTK implements
an annual review to continuously track the actual compliance of suppliers. We believe these
measures can help MTK reduce supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
As a fabless without manufacturing plants, MTK does not produce much wastewater compared
to manufacturing companies, and thus has relatively limited impact on the environment.
MTK has initiated water conservation methods, including efficient enhancement of using water-
saving apparatuses, installing rainwater recycling tanks and using recycled water in cooling
towers.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
MTK prioritises waste reduction, classification for reuse, adherence to recycling, and reutilisation
to manage waste efficiently and maximise the benefits of recycling. Also, it chooses only
qualified partners for waste disposal and recycling, and also audits the waste processes
regularly to ensure legal disposal of its waste and to fulfil its responsibility in waste management
supervision.
E GHG emissions 1
The main source for MTK’s GHG emissions is externally purchased electricity, which accounts
for over 90% of its total emissions. To enhance energy efficiency and reduce emissions, the
company implemented several measures, including ongoing improvements to data center
efficiency, full adoption of LED lighting in offices, and optimized operation of chiller units.
MediaTek continued advancing its renewable energy strategy and alignment with global climate
initiatives such as SBT and RE100. In early 2025, the Company passed the SBTi review and set
targets based on a 2020 baseline, including a 40% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 emissions by
2030, net zero by 2050, and achieving 100% renewable energy use across global offices
(excluding data centers) by 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

M
Update
Nuvoton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Losses Widen; BMC Growth and
Japan Restructuring Remain Key
3Q losses widened Q/Q amid lower gross profit, increased opex
and reduced non-operating income. Management sees scope for
faster BMC growth in 2027, with execution and Japan
restructuring critical to delivering earnings growth.
3Q losses widened: Revenue was NT$7.86bn, -1% Q/Q, +8% Y/Y, 3% above our
estimate; gross profit was NT$2.9bn, -3% Q/Q, +13% Y/Y, 6% above our forecast;

gross margin was 37.4%, -0.6ppt Q/Q, +1.7ppt Y/Y, 1.2ppt above our estimate. Loss
per share was NT$1.24, wider Q/Q, Y/Y, and vs. our expectation. Management
attributed gross margin weakness to product mix; back-end supply constraints and
substantial AI-related R&D left operational progress around 1Q behind plan.
Demand remains mixed: Auto demand has stabilized after prior Y/Y declines, but
management sees no clear rebound. PC demand was significantly weaker in 2H26

than in 1H26 with higher content per PC vs. 2025 helping cushion the impact of
lower volumes. Memory cost increases have been passed through on memory-
bundled MPUs, although some customers began pulling back in 4Q26. MCU
repricing reflects foundry and OSAT costs. Most 2027 capacity has been secured but
cost pass-through and supply availability do not establish margin expansion.
BMC and Japan remain the key milestones: Management sees scope for 2027 BMC

Y/Y growth to exceed 2026's, with existing customers and multiple new customers,
including a CSP, contributing. New customer revenue should initially be small with
fuller contributions over one to two years. Current-gen products should dominate
2027 shipments; next-gen volumes may start modestly in 2H27. Japan remains the
source of losses, making the Tower JV restructuring targeted by management for
end-March 2027 central to recovery.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(Y oY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(Y oY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(Y oY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(Y oY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(Y oY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(Y oY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

## Document 225: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.  
목표주가 (유지) 460,000원

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

 상대 24.4  20.4  (43.5)  
 절대 (달러환산) 6.1  13.8  (8.6)

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

## Document 268: AI资本开支见顶？高盛：拐点近了，AI正从“超级主题”变成“常规叙事”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI资本开支见顶？高盛：拐点近了，AI正从“超级主题”变成“常规叙事”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛判断， AI 资本开⽀的峰值增速已近在眼前，这⼀主题正从持续三年的 “ 超级充电叙事 ” ，
变成好坏两⾯都要算的 “ 常规故事 ” ，市场对其的定价已不再有单边⽀撑。与此同时，标普
500 和纳斯达克过去六个⽉基本原地踏步，本轮财报季 “ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃
突破⾏情 ” 。
摘要：
持续三年的 AI 超级周期叙事，或许正在悄然切换档位。⾼盛三位资深⼈⼠在最新⼀期播客对话中
判断： AI 资本开⽀的峰值增速已近在眼前，市场对 AI 主题的定价，已经从 “ 只往好处想 ” 的单边乐
观转向 “ 好坏两⾯都要算 ” 的双⾯博弈。⽽与此同时，美联储的实际加息幅度很可能低于市场当前
的定价预期。
录制于 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇的这期《 The Breaks of the Game 》节⽬中，⾼盛全球对冲基⾦覆盖主管 Tony
Pasquariello 、全球⻛险主管 Josh Schiffrin ，以及⾼盛研究部⾼级顾问 Dominic Wilson ，在超级
科技股财报周刚过之际，对 AI 资本开⽀叙事的演变、美联储路径及宏观⻛险墙进⾏了系统性复

盘。 Dominic Wilson 直⾔，AI 已从⼀个 “ 超级充电主题 ” 过渡到 “ 更常规的叙事 ” ，增⻓率的峰值
“ ⼤概率已相当接近 ” 。
这⼀判断的背景是：标普 500 指数在过去六个⽉基本原地踏步，纳斯达克同样如此。 Tony
Pasquariello 指出，本轮财报季 “ 表⾯上还不错 ” ，但前瞻盈利的上修幅度远不及此前 Q1 、 Q2 时
期，“ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃突破⾏情 ” 。
与此同时， Josh Schiffrin 将中东局势升级列为当前最⼤的近期增⻓⻛险，并将“ 做多全球前端利
率 ”列为年底最⾼确信度交易。
AI 从 “ 超级主题 ” 变成 “ 双向故事 ”
当⼀个主题被市场按 “ 最乐观路径 ” 定价时，只要出现⼀点不及预期的信息，负⾯冲击就会被放
⼤。⾼盛的判断是， AI 基础设施投资正处于⼀个临界点。
AI 交易⾛到这⼀步，是三条线索同时出现的结果。第⼀，市场价格已经包含了很乐观的预期；第
⼆，投资增速的峰值临近；第三，为这轮投资融资的信贷需求，开始在市场上留下痕迹。三条

叠在⼀起，这个主题就从 “ 信就⾏ ” 变成了 “ 得算账 ”—— 光靠信念不够，得看融资、看盈利、看
利率能不能撑住。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Dominic Wilson 在节⽬中拆解指出，⾃去年 6 ⽉ AI 相关板块出现那轮 “ 泡沫式峰值 ” 以来，该⾏⼀
直担忧市场对整个 AI 空间的定价已经过⾼ ——“ 不是说定价离谱，⽽是处于⼀个需要相当乐观的
路径才能⽀撑当前价格的⽔平，尤其是在基础设施领域 ” 。
⽽超级科技股财报季提供的数据，已不再是⼀边倒的信息。 Wilson 的原话是：“ 你在盈利端看到
的是⼀个散点图。我们⾃⼰的预测显⽰，投资冲动的峰值⼤概不会太远了。增⻓还会有，但峰
值增速⼤概率已相当接近。 ”
他进⼀步列举了三个具体的裂缝，包括融资和变现的疑问：其⼀，超级科技公司的信⽤融资需
求带来的信⽤利差压⼒ “ 时隐时现，但确实存在⼀定的跑输 ” ；其⼆，超级科技公司的变现路径上
“ 仍有问号 ” ；其三，盈利端的表现出现了分化，⽽⾮此前的整⻬划⼀。不过， Dominic 谈的是增
速峰值，没有说资本开⽀的绝对规模会下降。
Wilson 的结论是：“ ⼀旦你⾯对的是这种局⾯ —— 主要故事的增⻓率⻅顶、利率上⾏、信⽤利差

⾛阔 —— 感觉就像是⼀个更常规的叙事。它曾经是⼀个超级充电主题，持续了⼤概三年。现在
⻛险的天平更加两⾯，故事也更加常规。 ”
财报盈利托住了指数，却推不动指数
过去半年，美股的运⾏逻辑是⼀场拉锯：利率、能源以及⼀定程度上的信贷构成逆⻛，企业盈
利负责托底。结果是指数没有净位移。盈利季的问题不在于差，⽽在于 “ 刚好够⽤ ” ：业绩过了
关，但上调预期的幅度不⾜以让市场重新突破。
这是市场此刻最焦虑的地⽅。⼆季度财报期间，投资者曾意识到今年可能是盈利增⻓异常强劲
的⼀年，预期随之⼤幅上修。本轮财报没有重现这种效应。 Tony 说：“ 标普基本⼜回到了 7000
点 …… 纳斯达克这半年也没⾛出什么⽅向。 ”

### Page 3

“ 就这⼀季来看，盈利表⾯上还不错。⻔槛很⾼ …… 我觉得最有意思的是，前瞻盈利没有像⼀季
度、⼆季度财报期那样被上调。 ”
在他看来，全年盈利增⻓最终会很可观，可能达到接近 30% 甚⾄更⾼（按其所述⼝径，实录此
处⽂字记录不够清晰，发稿前建议核对⾳频）。但今年的数字已基本被市场消化。 Tony 的概括
是：“ 好到⾜以守住阵线，但还不⾜以添燃料，让市场再来⼀次突破。 ”
美联储：市场定价了太多，实际加息或⽌步于此
在 AI 叙事降温的同时，利率路径的不确定性构成了另⼀条主线。 Josh Schiffrin 对美联储 10 ⽉会
议的解读整体偏鸽，并明确认为市场当前的加息定价存在⾼估。
Schiffrin 的判断是：美联储⽬前认为⾃⼰有能⼒ “ 有条不紊地⾏动 ” 。尽管通胀形势依然严峻，但 9
⽉的政策⾏动已在产⽣效果，⾦融条件虽未收紧⾄极端⽔平，但已有所收紧。他的基准情景
是：12 ⽉再加息⼀次，此后或许再加⼀次，但 “ 最终的加息次数很可能少于市场当前的预期 ” 。
他的原话是： “ 你现在⼤约有 60 到 65 个基点的进⼀步加息被定价进去。我确实认为他们会在 12 ⽉

加息，但那很可能就是全部了。也许再多⼀次。但我的基准情景，就像我们上个⽉讨论的，是
12 ⽉再加⼀次。 ”
Schiffrin 同时指出，美联储从 2027 、 2028 年起将减少政策声明的频率，从每年⼋次会议缩减⾄
七次，但他认为这不会对市场产⽣重⼤影响： “ 有些会议本来就相隔很近，两次会议之间发⽣的
事情也不多，所以我不认为这会对市场产⽣很⼤影响。 ”

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Dominic Wilson 在利率问题上与 Schiffrin 的判断⾼度⼀致。他指出，此前利率上⾏的部分原因在
于， 10 年期远期 OIS 利率⼀度触及 5.20% 左右 ——“ 这已经⾮常接近过去 20 年政策利率周期的峰
值，如果你把这个作为 10 年后的中⼼点，那你已经建⽴了相当多的⻛险溢价 ” 。他认为， AI 叙事
的动摇也在通过 “ 增⻓担忧 ” 的渠道压低了收益率。
宏观⻛险墙：最⼤威胁来⾃中东，最被低估的是油价下⾏
在宏观⻛险的排序上，三位对话者的判断出现了⼀个有趣的分歧与互补： Josh Schiffrin 将中东
局势升级列为最⼤的近期⻛险，⽽ Dominic Wilson 则认为，油价 “ 不升级 ” 的尾部⻛险反⽽是当前
最被市场低估的机会。
Schiffrin 的逻辑直接：⼀旦冲突升级、能源价格再度上涨，这是最⼤的增⻓威胁；反之，若局势

缓和、油价回落， “ 这对资产整体有利，⽆论是股票还是债券 ” 。
Wilson 则从另⼀个⻆度切⼊。他认为，市场已经为伊朗地缘政治⻛险建⽴了相当多的⻛险溢
价，油市中存在真实的⻛险溢价。因此， “ 我们只是不跟进，或者升级程度不及市场担忧的那个
部分，我认为是被低估的 ” 。他的具体情景是：如果中期选举过后两三周内没有出现重⼤升级，
“ 市场会发现这⽐它所担忧的路径要好，因为我们已经建⽴了这种预期。即便没有任何实质性进
展，仅仅是没有新的坏消息，就已经⾜够了 ” 。
在年底最⾼确信度交易上， Schiffrin 明确指向“ 全球前端利率做多 ”—— 覆盖美国、欧洲和英国，
理由是各主要央⾏的实际⾏动将少于市场定价，⽽信⽤市场⼀旦出现波动，也会为前端利率提
供下⾏通道。
Tony Pasquariello 则对整体市场格局做出了总结性描述：过去六个⽉，宏观层⾯的利率、能源
和信⽤构成逆⻛，⽽盈利则 “ 守住了阵线 ” 。标普 500 和纳斯达克基本在原地踏步。本轮财报季 “ 表
⾯上还不错 ” ，但前瞻盈利的上修⼒度远不及 Q1 、 Q2 时期， “ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃

突破⾏情 ” 。他的结论是：如果 Schiffrin 和 Wilson 的判断正确，年底前股市或许能⾛出⼀段 “ 不错
的滑⾏路径 ” ，但可能是 “ 不同类型的股票 ” 在领涨。

## Document 285: BTIG警告：美股已进入“陌生地带”，黄金或进一步下探至3500美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/BTIG警告：美股已进入“陌生地带”，黄金或进一步下探至3500美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美股低波动实为板块极端分化制造的假象，内部结构极度脆弱。 BTIG 警告，市场亟需⼀场
恐慌性重挫洗盘，纳指⾯临⾄少 8.5% 的回撤，标普 500 下看 7300 点。此外，半导体已技术
破位， AI 板块或受⼤选政策反噬，⾦银均已筑顶，真正的底部远未到来。
摘要：
美股指数表⾯平静之下，内部正经历 2000 年以来罕⻅的剧烈撕裂。 BTIG Research 在 11 ⽉ 1 ⽇发
布的报告中警告，当前这场剧烈的板块轮动尚未结束，市场远未触及真正意义上的底部。
在过去五个交易⽇⾥，五个板块涨幅超过 2% ，同时两个板块跌幅超过 5% ，但 VIX 始终未能收于
20 上⽅ —— 这⼀看似⽭盾的组合，恰恰揭⽰了问题所在：波动率被横截⾯轮动所吸收，指数层
⾯的平静掩盖了内部结构的脆弱。 BTIG 认为，这种低 VIX 状态并不构成安全垫，相关性在下⾏
⽅向趋于⼀致的那⼀天尚未到来。
在关键位点上，标普 500 的⻛险下⾏⽬标指向 200 ⽇均线附近约 7300 点，费城半导体指数
（ SOXX ）的⻓期下⾏⽬标则落在 365 ⾄ 400 美元区间。与此同时，⻩⾦与⽩银已完成筑顶形

态， BTIG 对⻩⾦的下⾏⽬标为每盎司 3500 美元，⽩银为每盎司 40 美元。
表⾯波动率受抑，内部结构已然脆弱
报告称， VIX 未能收于 20 上⽅，在许多投资者眼中意味着市场尚在可控范围之内。但 BTIG 的分
析指向截然相反的结论。
五个板块单周涨幅超过 2% 、两个板块跌幅超过 5% ，这种极端的横截⾯分化意味着市场的真实波
动正在板块之间相互对冲，从⽽压低了指数层⾯的波动率读数。换⾔之， VIX 的低位并⾮市场稳
定的信号，⽽是轮动激烈程度的副产品 ——波动被横截⾯吸收，指数层⾯反⽽更加脆弱。
半导体板块的技术形态进⼀步印证了这⼀判断。等权重半导体 ETF （ XSD ）已第⼆次跌破 200 ⽇
均线，并逼近 7 ⽉低点的第三次测试。 BTIG 明确指出， “ 这不是底部应有的样⼦ ” 。费城半导体指
数（ SOXX ）同步破位， BTIG 认为其⻓期下⾏⽬标回归⾄ 4 ⽉突破点，即365 ⾄ 400 美元区间。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

历史形态显⽰：情绪远未洗清，下跌或未结束
BTIG 援引多项历史数据，论证当前市场情绪与技术结构均不⽀持 " 已经⻅底 " 的判断。
纳斯达克 100 指数出现了⾃ 1985 年以来第⼋次在历史⾼位形成负外包⽉形态。从历史经验看，前
七次发⽣后六个⽉均录得正回报，这对多头⽽⾔是⼀个安慰。但另⼀⾯同样不容忽视：前七次
⽆⼀例外地在随后六个⽉内出现⾄少 8.5% 的回撤，其中 1990 年和 2020 年的两次回撤幅度更超过
20% 。
情绪指标同样显⽰洗盘远未完成。纳斯达克每⽇情绪指数（ DSI ）⽬前收于 44 ，为 7 ⽉以来最
低，但7 ⽉的低点是 30 ， 3 ⽉的低点是 12—— 当前 44 的读数距离真正的恐慌性低点仍有相当距
离。
更关键的是，近⼀年内纽约证券交易所从未出现过单⽇下跌成交量占⽐超过 80% 的交易⽇，
BTIG 将这⼀现象描述为 " 史⽆前例 " 。在其看来，在出现⾄少⼀次、甚⾄数次这样的 " 相关性趋
⼀ " 式重挫之前，市场尚不具备重新进攻的条件。
信⽤市场也在发出警⽰。⾼收益债平均利差已升⾄ 340 个基点，创年内新⾼。此前市场担忧主要

集中于 CCC 级债券，但如今平均利差的⾛阔表明压⼒已向更⼴泛的信⽤市场蔓延。
关键位点：标普 500 看向 7300 ，防守端⻛险回报已变
在具体的⻛险位点上， BTIG 将标普 500 的下⾏⽬标锁定在 200 ⽇均线附近约 7300 点，即夏季低点
⼀带。这⼀位置将是多空双⽅的关键博弈区域。
防守性板块的⻛险回报格局也在悄然转变。必需消费品板块刚刚录得2000 年 10 ⽉以来最强单⽉
表现，⽉涨幅超过 11%，资⾦避险属性的集中释放令该板块短期性价⽐下降。公⽤事业板块同样
如此 ——BTIG 在 9 ⽉极度超卖时看好该板块，但在经历 11% 的反弹并触及关键阻⼒位后，其⻛险
回报已完全逆转，不再具备此前的吸引⼒。
贵⾦属⽅⾯， BTIG 认为⻩⾦与⽩银均已完成筑顶形态，维持谨慎⽴场，下⾏⽬标分别为⻩⾦每
盎司 3500 美元、⽩银每盎司 40 美元。
中期选举变量：堪萨斯翻蓝， AI 板块⾯临政策逆⻛
政治层⾯的不确定性为市场增添了新的变量。 BTIG 指出，⺠调与博彩市场的预测常常出错，但

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports. 1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

4 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026, 2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.
A) Higher 10-year Yield Consistent with Strong Nominal Growth Environment. B) Domestic Private Investors Now Dominate U.S. Treasury Market.

yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.

## Document 304: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

## Document 319: DeepSeek上新模式强化智能体能力：一句话定制AI插件，公开提示词搞定3D可视化到视频编辑

Source PDF: all report pdf/DeepSeek上新模式强化智能体能力：一句话定制AI插件，公开提示词搞定3D可视化到视频编辑.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

以 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” 为核⼼理念的开源智能体框架 DeepSeek Harness 推出 “ 创作者模式 ” ，可⼀
句话⽣成插件，发布 7 组中英⽂演⽰提⽰词，覆盖 3D 建筑展⽰、 Transformer 运算可视化、
应⽤界⾯定制、海报设计、视频编辑等场景。
摘要：
DeepSeek 正在进⼀步将 AI 智能体（ Agent ）的能⼒从执⾏任务拓展到⾃主构建⼯具。
11 ⽉ 2 ⽇周⼀， DeepSeek Harness 团队宣布推出全新的 “ 创作者模式 ” （ Creator Mode ），⽤⼾只
需通过⾃然语⾔描述需求，即可将想法转化为可安装、可使⽤的⾃定义插件，扩展 Harness 的⼯
具、技能和交互界⾯。
DeepSeek 在社交媒体上以 “ 你的 Harness ，由你定义 ” （ Your Harness. You Define It ）为⼝号介
绍这⼀功能，并展⽰了设计⼯作台、沙盒游戏和 3D ⼤语⾔模型可视化⼯具等应⽤⽅向。官⽅同
时发布了 7 组中英⽂演⽰提⽰词，覆盖 3D 建筑展⽰、 Transformer 运算可视化、应⽤界⾯定制、

海报设计、视频编辑等场景。
这⼀更新的重点不只是让 AI ⽣成代码，⽽是试图让⽤⼾通过对话直接创建能够融⼊ Harness 运⾏
环境的功能插件，进⼀步降低定制智能体⼯作流的⻔槛。
从⼀句话⽣成插件，官⽅公布 7 组演⽰案例
从官⽅公布的提⽰词合集来看， Creator Mode 并⾮局限于⽣成简单的⼩⼯具，⽽是试图覆盖从
交互式应⽤、专业⼯作台到数据分析界⾯的多种需求。
7 组演⽰案例分别为：
3D 地标建筑展厅：创建独⽴插件，以 3D 模型展⽰巴黎埃菲尔铁塔、中国⻓城、紫禁城等著名
建筑。
Transformer 三维可视化：制作交互式 3D 演⽰游戏，帮助⽤⼾理解 Transformer 内部的计算过
程。
应⽤像素⻛界⾯：允许⽤⼾切换整个 Harness 应⽤的视觉⻛格，提供五种⻛格选择，其中包
括像素⻛。
海报设计⼯作台：在会话中直接调⽤设计插件，⽀持⾃定义海报标题、正⽂和背景 Shader 颜
⾊。
视频编辑⼯作台：提供类似专业剪辑软件的深⾊界⾯，⽀持多视频上传、预览、时间轴剪辑

和导出，还可以直接通过⾃然语⾔指令完成导⼊和编辑。
论⽂阅读器：⽀持上传论⽂并保留原始排版、字体和图⽚，⽤⼾选中⽂字后，可以进⾏⾼
亮、添加笔记，或直接在对话中提问。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

会话统计⼯具：基于⽤⼾真实对话数据，展⽰ Token 总消耗、会话数量、成本预算、 Token 使
⽤时间线、单次会话⽤量及 CPU 活动和利⽤率。
这 7 组提⽰词的共同点是，⽤⼾不必先掌握插件开发流程，⽽是可以直接描述希望实现的功能、
界⾯和交互⽅式，由 Harness 尝试将其转化为插件。
不过， DeepSeek 也在官⽅提⽰词合集中特别说明，部分演⽰插件经过了多轮对话调整，因此仅
凭⼀条提⽰词，不⼀定能够完全复现视频中的最终效果。这意味着 Creator Mode 降低的是开发
和定制的起点⻔槛，并不意味着复杂应⽤已经能够通过⼀次指令稳定完成。
从 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” 到⽤⼾⾃主扩展
Creator Mode 背后，是 DeepSeek Harness 此前确⽴的插件化架构。
DeepSeek Harness 是⼀款⾯向开发者和专业⽤⼾的开源智能体框架，基于 Cordis 构建，其核⼼
理念是 “ ⼀切皆插件 ” （ Everything is a plugin ）。模型、⼯具、技能以及界⾯等能⼒均可以通过插

件进⾏扩展和组合，⽽不是被固定在单⼀的软件功能框架内。
DeepSeek 官⽹⽬前将 Harness 定位为⾯向全球开放测试的开源产品，⽀持桌⾯应⽤和⽹⻚端运
⾏。除了编写代码、分析数据、整理⽂件和研究资料，⽤⼾还可以安装现有插件，或者在
Creator Mode 中通过对话创建⾃⼰的插件，以扩展⼯具、技能和交互界⾯。
从官⽅展⽰的实际开发过程来看，⽤⼾提出创建番茄时钟插件的需求后， Harness 会加载 Cordis
插件开发技能，检查当前运⾏环境和可⽤接⼝，编写插件配置及代码，再调⽤插件管理⼯具安
装，并进⼀步检查插件是否正常运⾏。官⽅演⽰中，⼀个悬浮式番茄时钟插件从开发到安装验
证耗时约 5 分 24 秒。
这也体现出 Creator Mode 与普通聊天式代码⽣成的区别：它不只是输出⼀段代码或⼀个独⽴⽹
⻚，⽽是试图让智能体直接参与⾃⾝运⾏环境的扩展，将⽣成的功能接⼊ Harness 现有的插件体
系。
AI 智能体竞争从模型能⼒延伸⾄⼯具⽣态
Creator Mode 的推出，也反映出 AI ⾏业竞争重⼼的进⼀步变化：随着⼤模型逐渐具备更强的代

码⽣成和⼯具调⽤能⼒，如何让智能体在真实⼯作环境中持续执⾏任务、调⽤⼯具并扩展⾃⾝
能⼒，正在成为新的竞争⽅向。
据此前报道， DeepSeek 今年早些时候组建 Harness 团队，重点开发能够与美国 AI 公司的编程智
能体竞争的产品。 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 等产品，正是这⼀市场的重要竞争者。 DeepSeek 希
望通过 Harness 进⼀步拓展 AI 智能体在代码开发和专业⼯作中的应⽤。
从这个⻆度看， Creator Mode 并⾮⼀次单纯的界⾯升级，⽽是 DeepSeek 在智能体产品⽣态上的
进⼀步布局。
传统 AI 编程⼯具主要解决的是如何让模型帮助⽤⼾编写代码、修改程序，⽽ Creator Mode 试图
进⼀步解决如何让⽤⼾利⽤ AI 直接扩展智能体本⾝的能⼒。⽤⼾既可以让 Harness 完成⼀项任
务，也可以要求它创建⼀个能够反复使⽤的新⼯具，甚⾄定制整个应⽤的交互界⾯。
这种模式如果能够成熟，意味着 AI 智能体的产品边界将不再完全由开发团队预先设定，⽽是可以

随着⽤⼾需求不断扩展。对 DeepSeek ⽽⾔，开放插件开发也有助于吸引更多开发者参与⽣态建

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 336: Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone _ Growth on track despite the turbulence (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

8
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
GROWTH ON TRACK DESPITE THE TURBULENCE
Growth was stronger than expected in Q2 and is set to strengthen further in the coming quarters. According to our fore-
casts, it will reach 1.5% in 2027 after 1.1% in 2026. This growth is expected to be driven by investment and its knock-on
effect on exports. On an annual average basis, inflation is expected to stand at 3% in 2026 and 2.8% in 2027, peaking at

the end of 2026. The ECB is expected to continue its monetary tightening. At this stage, a single further rate rise, aimed at
keeping inflation expectations anchored, is expected in December. The impact of rising interest rates (key policy rates and
market rates) on public finances and private sector borrowing conditions will be worth monitoring.
GROWTH: BEYOND THE SHOCKS, A RESILIENT MOMENTUM
Growth in the Eurozone remained robust in the first half of 2026, at

0.25% q/q in Q1 and 0.33% q/q in Q2, excluding Ireland (where economic
activity is volatile). It was driven by Germany, where signs of a recovery
are becoming clearer (see Germany). Our baseline scenario forecasts
growth of 1.1% in 2026, followed by 1.5% in 2027 on an annual average
basis. This growth is expected to be driven by the strengthening of
trends already underway in public and private investment (particularly
in defence and AI) and in exports (trade in services and intra-zone trade

in goods). Household consumption is likely to be resilient too. According
to our forecasts, job creation would continue to underpin household
incomes, thereby cushioning the impact of inflation.
A NEW LEASE OF LIFE FOR INDUSTRY
The Eurozone is undergoing one of the most profound industrial trans-
formations in its history. This is giving rise to divergences between key
sectors but, above all, to new drivers of growth whose effects are beco-
ming increasingly apparent (see our analysis). Indeed, the signs of an

economic recovery are growing stronger. Business sentiment indicators
show a marked improvement in the economic outlook across most sec-
tors (Chart 2), driven primarily by electronics (AI, digitalisation), the
automotive sector and aerospace. European industry is thus benefi -
ting from strong demand for capital goods; a structural trend that will
continue to be fuelled by ongoing investment cycles in AI, defence and
electrification. The introduction of minimum European content quotas

in the draft law on the industrial accelerator, together with the Com -
mission’s intention to tighten the criteria for access to European public
procurement markets, will provide additional support for European in-
dustry in the longer term.
FOREIGN TRADE: A REPOSITIONING THAT IS PAYING OFF
Following a decline in 2026, linked to rising energy import costs, the
trade surplus is expected to rise to around 3.0% of GDP in 2027 (of
which 2% of GDP for goods and 1% for services). Growth in European ex-

ports has slowed compared with its pre-Covid trend and is being ham-
pered by the decline in Chinese and US markets. However, Eurozone
exports in volume rose significantly in Q2 (+3.4% q/q).
More importantly, since Liberation Day (April 2025), Eurozone coun -
tries have found significant sources of growth amongst other European
partners, which has enabled them to cushion the decline in exports to
certain third markets. This is evident in exports to the rest of the EU,

EFTA countries (Switzerland in particular) and, to a lesser extent, the
United Kingdom, Ukraine and the Balkans (Chart 3). The momentum of
intra-European trade therefore remains very strong. It is expected to
strengthen further with the anticipated investments in defence, AI and
electrification.
FINANCING: FINANCIAL CONDITIONS ARE EXPECTED TO REMAIN TIGHT
Financial conditions in the Eurozone remained tight in Q2 2026, accor-
ding to indices calculated by the ECB (including the BIG [Broad Inter -

mediation Gauge]). They are expected to tighten more significantly in
the second half of the year: i/ the rise in long-term bond yields, which
has accelerated since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran, is expected to
continue; and ii/ the ECB will continue its monetary tightening (+25 bps
